WCI — exact rep responses to every caller question, batched by similarity, with frequency and sales-rep breakdown

Built 2026-09-04 17:34 · 19,933 exact rep responses located in 4,898 de-identified call transcripts (1,060 question quotes could not be located and are excluded) · reps = opaque extension ids 184223, 195705, 555999, 306441, 175815 · won/lost ignored · every sentence shown passed the HIPAA scanner; names/numbers appear as [NAME]/[PHONE]/[DATE] placeholders.
How to read. For each caller question, the rep’s exact words in the turn(s) right after it. Similar responses are batched (same content, not word-for-word); each batch shows its central wording first, then up to two other exact wordings from the same batch, the count, the % of answers to that question, the count per rep, and the model’s on-script judgement. MOST COMMON = the de facto script line for that question. Responses said only once are counted in the last row of each table but not printed.
